Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The milky_way operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 164. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 564 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to milky_way structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 164/564 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the milky_way structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 646. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 351 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Theorem 222.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
